                                                    Corporate Introduction
                                                          公司簡介

Kingdee International Software Group Company Limited ("Kingdee International" or "Kingdee") was founded in 1993 and is listed on the Main Board of
the Hong Kong Stock Exchange (stock code: 268.HK). Headquartered in Shenzhen, China, Kingdee is a globally leading and China's No. 1 enterprise
management cloud SaaS company. Guided by its core values of "Act with conscience, walk the righteous path, lead with benevolence" and its mission
to "Empower every enterprise to achieve phenomenal success," Kingdee is committed to becoming "the most trustworthy AI platform for enterprise
management." Over more than 30 years, Kingdee has provided products and services to over 7.4 million enterprises and government organizations
worldwide, and is the common choice for numerous Fortune 500 companies and "Little Giant" enterprises. Kingdee has received extensive recognition from
renowned domestic and international research institutions in cloud services and AI: according to IDC and Gartner data, Kingdee has maintained the No. 1
market share in China's enterprise resource management cloud and aPaaS markets for multiple years, and is the only enterprise management vendor among
the Top 10 Generative AI Model (Gen AI model) vendors in the China market.

金蝶國際軟件集團有限公司（「金蝶國際」或「金蝶」）始創於1993 年，是香港聯交所主板上市公司（股票代碼：268.HK），總
部位於中國深圳，是全球領先、中國第一的企業管理雲SaaS公司。以「致良知、走正道、行王道」為核心價值觀，以「予力
企業，成就不凡」為使命，致力成為「最值得託付的企業管理AI平台」。30多年來，金蝶已為世界範圍內超過740萬家企業、
政府組織提供產品及服務，是眾多500強企業及專精特新企業的共同選擇。金蝶在雲服務和AI領域屢獲國內外知名研究機
構認可：IDC及Gartner數據顯示，金蝶在中國企業資源管理雲及aPaaS市場多年穩居市占率第一，也是生成式AI模型（Gen AI
model）中國市場TOP10中唯一的企業管理廠商。

                                                                   二零二五年年報             金蝶國際軟件集團有限公司                        7
CHAIRMAN’S STATEMENT
主席報告

2025 marks a pivotal turning point in Kingdee’s history. The
subscription transition has achieved a high-quality turnaround
to profitability, with Net Dollar Retention (NDR) reaching a three-
year peak and net operating cash flow hitting an all-time high.
These results definitively validate the success of Kingdee’s cloud
transformation. Meanwhile, the accelerated AI transformation and
global expansion are unlocking new horizons for the next phase of
growth.

2025年是金蝶發展史上的關鍵轉折點：訂閱模式實
現高質量扭虧為盈，淨金額續費率為近三年最高，
經營活動產生淨現金創歷史新高，業績證明金蝶的
雲轉型已經取得成功；同時，全面加速AI轉型與全
球化佈局，為下一階段的增長打開全新空間。
Chairman’s Statement
主席報告



Cloud Success, Achieving Profitability via Subscription                                        雲轉型取得成功，用訂閱模式實現盈利

Over the past five years, we have steadfastly pursued our cloud subscription transformation.   過去五年，我們堅定不移地推進雲訂閱轉型。2025
In 2025, Group revenue grew 12% year-on-year to RMB7,006 million, with cloud service           年，這 份 堅 持 開 花 結 果。集 團 收 入 同 比 增 長12%至
revenue accounting for 82.5% of total revenue. Subscription Annual Recurring Revenue           70.06億 元，雲 服 務 收 入 占 比 達82.5%，訂 閱ARR同 比
(ARR) increased approximately 19.2% year-on-year to RMB4,090 million. Our growth               增長約19.2%至40.9億元，我們的增長是建立在可持
is built on a sustainable and predictable business model, and the subscription model           續、可預測的業務模式之上，訂閱模式正在釋放其
is increasingly releasing its economies of scale. The Group achieved a comprehensive           規模效應。集團實現全面扭虧為盈，實現歸屬於股
turnaround from loss to profit, delivering net profit attributable to shareholders of RMB93    東權益淨利潤0.93億元，經調整後淨利潤2.32億元；
million and adjusted net profit of RMB232 million; Cash inflow from operating activities       經營活動產生淨現金達到10.97億元，現金儲備淨額
reached RMB1,097 million, with Group’s net cash reserves approaching RMB5 billion,             近50億元，持續支撐集團未來的研發投入和全球化
providing sustained support for future R&D investment and global expansion.                    擴張。

for the 21st consecutive year, ranking first across multiple sub-segments including SaaS       軍，並在SaaS ERP、財務雲、司庫雲、稅務雲等多個

Embracing Change, Accelerating AI Transformation                                               擁抱變革，加速AI轉型

In 2025, we formally upgraded “Kingdee Cloud” to “Kingdee AI.” This is not merely a            2025年，我們將「金蝶雲」正式升級為「金蝶AI」。這
brand evolution, but a clear strategic declaration: AI is fundamentally changing the user      不僅是品牌的跨越，更是一個清晰的戰略宣示：AI
experience, technical architecture, and business model of enterprise software, and Kingdee     正在從根本上改變企業軟件的用戶體驗、技術架構
is fully embracing this transformation. We launched our super portal “Xiao K” and nearly       和商業模式，金蝶將全面擁抱這場變革。我們發佈
20 AI-native intelligent agents. Operational analysis that previously required days to         了超級入口「小K」及近20款AI原生智能體，以往需要
complete can now be accomplished in minutes, with costs reduced by over 70%. The               數天完成的經營分析現已縮短至分鐘級，成本降低
true moat for AI lies not in algorithms, but in industry knowledge. Kingdee’s over thirty      70%以 上。而AI真 正 的 壁 壘 不 在 算 法，在 於 行 業 知
years of service to more than 7.4 million enterprises have accumulated multi-industry          識。金蝶三十餘年服務超740萬家企業所沉澱的多行
financial and tax compliance systems, global supply chain management expertise, and            業財稅合規體系、全球供應鏈管理及複雜製造Know-
complex manufacturing Know-How – all of which are now being transformed into reusable          How，正在被轉化為可複用的知識模型－這是AI無法
knowledge models. This is a core capability that AI cannot generate from scratch, and it is    憑空生成的核心能力，也是金蝶最深的護城河。
Kingdee’s deepest competitive moat.

Global Expansion: Sharing Chinese Management Wisdom                                               全球化佈局，中國管理智慧走向世界
Worldwide

The globalization of Chinese enterprises is accelerating, and Kingdee’s mission extends           中國企業的全球化正在加速，金蝶的使命也隨之延
with it. In 2025, the Group is fast-tracking its global footprint by establishing local service   展。集團加速全球化佈局，已在卡塔爾、越南、泰

Connected Transactions                                                                         關連交易

(I)    Equity Transfer Agreement between Hongjin Investment and                                （一）弘金投資与蝶創控股關於收購雲之家的股
       Diechuang Holdings in relation to the Acquisition of Equity Interest                       權轉讓協議
       in CloudHub

       On 15 September 2025, the Group, through Shenzhen Hongjin Investment                       於二零二五年九月十五日，本集團通過深圳
       Company Limited (“Hongjin Investment”) (as purchaser) and Diechuang                        市弘金投資有限公司（「弘金投資」）           （作為買方）
       Holdings Limited (“Diechuang Holdings”) (a company owned as to 99%                         與蝶創控股有限公司（「蝶創控股」）            （徐少春先
       by Mr. Xu Shao Chun) (as seller), entered into the Equity Transfer Agreement,              生擁有99%股份的公司）      （作為賣方）訂立股權
       pursuant to which Hongjin Investment agreed to purchase 62.764% of the equity              轉 讓 協 議，據 此，弘 金 投 資 同 意 購 買 蝶 創 控
       interest in CloudHub Network (Chongqing) Co., Ltd. (formerly known as Shenzhen             股持有的雲之家網絡（重慶）有限公司（原名
       CloudHub Network Co., Ltd.) (“CloudHub”) held by Diechuang Holdings                        深圳雲之家網絡有限公司）         （「雲之家」）62.764%
       (the “Acquisition”). As Diechuang Holdings is an associate of Mr. Xu, the                  的股權（「收購」）。由於蝶創控股為徐先生的
       Acquisition constituted a connected transaction. The total consideration for the           聯 繫 人，因 此 該 收 購 事 項 構 成 關 連 交 易。收
       Acquisition is RMB68,250,000, which was determined after taking into account               購 事 項 的 总 代 价 為 人 民 幣68,250,000元，該 代
       the valuation of CloudHub of RMB108,745,000 as at 30 April 2025 as appraised               價考慮了獨立估值師艾華迪評估諮詢有限公
       by an independent valuer, AVISTA Valuation Advisory Limited, using the market              司採用市場法所評估的目標公司於二零二五
       approach. Upon completion of the Acquisition, the Group’s equity interest in               年四月三十日的估值金額為人民幣108,745,000

Shareholders. The details of the corporate governance practices of the Company are set

       responsibilities are as follows:                                                        責如下：

       (1)     to convene general meetings, report to Shareholders and implement the           (1)   召集股東大會、向股東大會報告及執行

Board Evaluation                                                                                董事會評估

During the Reporting Period, the Company conducted a formal evaluation of the Board's           本公司於報告期內進行了2025年度董事會表現正式
performance for the year 2025. The evaluation was carried out by the International Legal        評核。由國際法務部通過問卷調查及個別面談的形
Department through questionnaires and individual interviews, covering the Board's               式進行。涵蓋董事會結構及技能組合、決策流程的
structure and skill mix, the transparency of decision-making processes, the effectiveness       透明度、對管理層的監督有效性、風險監控以及董
of supervision over management, risk monitoring, and the Board's guiding role in the            事會對公司AI轉型戰略的指導作用。
Company's AI transformation strategy.

The evaluation results indicate that the Board has been operating effectively overall. Key      評核結果顯示董事會運作整體有效。主要優勢包括
strengths include a skill mix highly aligned with the Company's strategy, a balanced            技能組合高度契合策略、學術與產業權威並重、獨
representation of academic and industry authorities, and a sound framework for                  立性與監督職能健全。
independence and oversight functions. The evaluation identified room for improvement            評核發現在董事會多元化的量化目標設定以及董事
in setting quantitative targets for Board diversity and recording the hours of continuous       持續培訓的時數記錄方面仍有提升空間。為此，董
training for Directors. To address these areas, the Board plans to take the following actions   事會計劃於2026年：
in 2026:

(1)    Establish a more detailed director training log to record the specific hours and         (1)   建立更詳細的董事培訓日誌，記錄每名董事
       topics of continuous professional development for each Director; and                           參與持續專業發展的具體時數與主題。

(2)    Further strengthen the quantitative monitoring of ESG risks by the strategy and          (2)    進一步加強本公司戰略與可持續發展委員會
       sustainable development committee of the Company (the “Strategy and Sustainable                （「戰略與可持續發展委員會」）對ESG風險的量
       Development Committee”).                                                                        化監控。

Board Committees                                                                                董事會委員會

The Board has set up four specialized committees, namely the audit committee of the             董事會設立了四個專門委員會，包括本公司審核委

Board Diversity Policy                                                                      董事會多元化政策

The Company has adopted a board diversity policy (the “Board Diversity Policy”) which       本公司已採納董事會成員多元化（「董事會多元化政
sets out the approach to achieve diversity in the Board, which will include and make good   策」）政策。政策列載董事會為達致成員多元化而採
use of the differences in skills, backgrounds and industry experience, gender, knowledge    取的方針，其中將涵蓋且善用董事會各成員所具備
and other qualities of the members of the Board. These differences will be considered in    不同的技能、背景及行業經驗、性別、知識以及其
determining the optimum composition of the Board and all Board appointments will be         他特質。在組成最理想的董事會時，將考慮上述各
based on merits, having due regard to the overall effective functioning of the Board as a   方面的差異，而董事會所有委任均以用人唯才為原
whole. The Company believes that diversity can strengthen the performance of the Board,     則，且顧及到董事會整體的職能可有效發揮。本公
promote effective decision-making and better corporate governance and monitoring. The       司深信，成員多元化可提高董事會的績效、促進有
Nomination Committee also monitors the implementation of the Board Diversity Policy         效的決策，以及嚴謹的企業管治和監察。提名委員
and reports to the Board on the achievement of the measurable objectives for achieving      會亦會監察政策的實行，並按照董事會多元化政策
diversity under this policy. All Board appointments are made on a merit basis with due      的規定，向董事會匯報達致成員多元化的可計量目
regard to the benefits of diversity of the Board members. Selection of candidates will be   標有否達成。董事會所有的委任均以有利於董事會
based on a range of diversity considerations, including but not limited to gender, age,     成員多元化為原則。候選人之選擇從一系列多元化
cultural and educational background, experience (professional or otherwise), skills and     視角出發，包括但不限於性別、年齡、文化、教育
knowledge. The ultimate decision will be made based on the merits and contributions that    背景、經驗（專業或其他方面）、技能及知識水準。
the selected candidates will bring to the Board. At present, two of the seven members of    選定候選人最終決定於其未來對本公司董事會的貢

      The Company has designed its major risk management criteria, including risk                  本 公 司 設 計 了 風 險 識 別、風 險 評 估、風 險 應
      identification, risk assessment, risk response and risk control and reporting:               對、風險監控與報告主要風險管理環節：

      •      Risk identification: The Audit Committee reviews the effectiveness of the             •   風險識別：審核委員會每年檢討本集團

             urge all business units and functional departments to identify the internal               業務單元與職能部門識別營運過程中可
             and external risks that may exist during the course of operation;                         能存在的內外部風險；

      •      Risk assessment: All business units and functional departments must assess            •   風險評估：各業務單元與職能部門對識
             the possibility of the occurrence of identified risks and the extent of their             別的風險，從發生的可能性及影響程度
             impact and screen out major risks;                                                        進行評價，篩選出重大風險；

      •      Risk response: The risk responsibility department properly uses risk                  •   風險應對：風險責任部門對已識別的重
             avoidance, reduction, sharing or tolerance measures to draw up a risk                     大風險，結合本集團風險承受度，妥善
             response scheme according to identified major risks and the Group’s                       使用風險迴避、降低、分擔或承受等方
             risk tolerance, so that the Group can reasonably allocate resources for risk              法制定風險應對方案，以促使本集團合
             response and reduce its overall risk level to an acceptable level; and                    理調配資源應對風險，使本集團的整體
                                                                                                       風險水準降至可接受範圍；及

      •      Risk control and reporting: The Group’s risks are controlled and reported             •   風險監控與報告：綜合利用風險預警指
             through the comprehensive use of risk pre-warning indicators, internal                    標、內部審計、定期風險總結報告等形

be communicated in our report because the adverse consequences of doing so would           事項造成的負面後果超過產生的公眾利益，我們決
reasonably be expected to outweigh the public interest benefits of such communication.     定不應在報告中溝通該事項。

The engagement partner on the audit resulting in this independent auditor’s report is      出具本獨立核數師報告的審計項目合夥人是王賦成。
Wong Fu Shing.

NOTES TO THE CONSOLIDATED FINANCIAL STATEMENTS

1    General information                                                                 1   一般資料

     Kingdee International Software Group Company Limited (the “Company”)                    金 蝶 國 際 軟 件 集 團 有 限 公 司（「本 公 司」）於
     was incorporated in the Cayman Islands in 1999 as an exempted company with              一九九九年在開曼群島註冊成立為獲豁免有
     limited liability. The address of its place of business is Kingdee Software Park,       限公司。其營業地點為中華人民共和國（「中
     No.2 Kejinan 12 Road, South district, Hi-tech Industrial Park, Nanshan District,        國」）廣東省深圳市南山區高新技術產業園區
     Shenzhen, Guangdong Province, the People’s Republic of China (the “PRC”).               科技南十二路2號，金蝶軟件園。

     The Company is an investment holding company. The Company and its                       本公司為一家投資控股公司。本公司及其附
     subsidiaries (together the “Group”) are principally engaged in the subscription         屬公司（統稱「本集團」）的主要業務包括訂閱

       losses which uses a lifetime expected loss allowance for all trade receivables and            法計量預期信用損失，即對所有應收賬款及
       contract assets.                                                                              合同資產確認整個存續期的預期損失撥備。

       To measure the expected credit losses, trade receivables and contract assets have             為了衡量預期的信用損失，已根據共同的信
       been grouped based on shared credit risk characteristics and the invoice days. The            用風險特徵和賬齡對應收賬款和合同資產進
       contract assets relate to unbilled work in progress and have substantially the same           行了分組。合同資產與未開票的尚在進行中
       risk characteristics as the trade receivables within aging bucket for the same types          的業務相關，並且具有與相同類型合同應收
       of contracts. The Group has therefore concluded that the expected loss rates for              賬 款 實 質 上 相 同 的 風 險 特 徵。因 此，本 集 團
       trade receivables within aging bucket are a reasonable approximation of the loss              得出結論，賬齡時段內應收賬款的預期損失
       rates for the contract assets.                                                                率是合同資產損失率的合理近似值。

(c)    Liquidity risk                                                                          (c)    流動性風險

       The Group exercises prudent liquidity risk management by reviewing forecast cash               本集團將於每季度審閱預期現金餘額，以此
       balances on a quarterly basis to maintain sufficient cash levels and the availability          執行穩健的流動資金管理政策，維持足夠的
       of an adequate amount of committed credit facilities to ensure the availability of             現金量及有效可用的信貸額度以確保足夠現

3.3   Capital risk management                                                                3.3   資金風險管理

      The Group’s objectives when managing capital are to safeguard the Group’s                    本集團的資金管理政策，是保障集團能持續
      ability to continue as a going concern in order to provide returns for owners and            運營，以為股東提供回報和為其他權利持有
      benefits for other stakeholders and to maintain an optimal capital structure to              人提供利益，同時維持最佳的資本結構以降
      reduce the cost of capital.                                                                  低資金成本。

      In order to maintain or adjust the capital structure, the Group may adjust the               為了維持或調整資本結構，本集團可能會調
      amount of dividends paid to owners, return capital to shareholders, issue new                整支付予股東的股利數額、向股東退還資本、
      shares or sell assets to reduce debt.                                                        發行新股或出售資產以減低債務。

      Consistent with others in the industry, the Group monitors capital on the basis              與業內其他公司一樣，本集團利用資本負債
      of the gearing ratio. This ratio is calculated as net debt divided by total capital.         比率監察其資本。此比率按照債務淨額除以
      Net debt is calculated as total borrowings (including “current and non-current               總資本計算。債務淨額為總借款（包括綜合財
      borrowings” and “current and non-current lease liabilities” as shown in the                  務狀況表所列的「流動及非流動借款」和「流
      consolidated statement of financial position) less cash and cash equivalents. Total          動及非流動租賃負債」）減去現金和現金等價
      capital is calculated as “equity” as shown in the consolidated statement of                  物。總資本為「權益」
                                                                                                            （如綜合財務狀況表所列）
      financial position plus net debt.                                                            加債務淨額。

      The gearing ratios at 31 December 2025 and 2024 were as follows:                             在二零二五年及二零二四年十二月三十一日，

4     Critical accounting estimates and judgments                                              4     關鍵會計估計及判斷

      Estimates and judgments are continually evaluated and are based on historical                  估計和判斷會被持續評估，並根據過往經驗
      experience and other factors, including expectations of future events that are                 和其他因素進行評價，包括在有關情況下相
      believed to be reasonable under the circumstances.                                             信對未來事件的合理預測。

      The Group makes estimates and assumptions concerning the future. The resulting                 本集團對未來作出估計和假設。所得的會計
      accounting estimates will, by definition, seldom equal the related actual results.             估 計 如 其 定 義，很 少 會 與 其 實 際 結 果 相 同。
      The estimates and assumptions that have a significant risk of causing a material               很大機會導致下個財政年度的資產和負債的
      adjustment to the carrying amounts of assets and liabilities within the next financial         賬面值作出重大調整的估計和假設討論如下。
      year are addressed below.

      the customers. For the year ended 31 December 2025, revenue of approximately                   實 施 合 同 確 認 了 約 人 民 幣1,891,905,000元（二

      If the progress of work performed were to differ by 10% from management’s                      層 的 估 計 出 現10%的 差 異（比 如：33%而 不 是

4.2   Estimated impairment of goodwill                                                         4.2   商譽的估計減值

      The Group tests annually whether goodwill has suffered any impairment in                       根據附註8所述的會計政策，本集團每年測試
      accordance with the accounting policy stated in Note 8. For the purpose of assessing           商 譽 是 否 出 現 減 值。為 評 估 減 值，本 集 團 認
      impairment, the Group considered that each acquired subsidiary is a separate                   為被評估單位是獨立的現金產出單位（「CGU」）
      group of cash-generating-unit (the “CGU”) and the goodwill is allocated to                     組合，並且已向CGU分配商譽。現金產出單元
      the CGU. Management assessed the impairment of goodwill by determining the                     的可收回金額基於其公允價值減去處置成本
      recoverable amount of the CGU, representing the higher of its fair value less cost             與使用價值兩者的較高者確定，其按基於管
      of disposal and value-in-use which is calculated using cash flow forecasts based               理層批准的財務預算的現金流預測而計算。
      on the financial budgets approved by management. The goodwill impairment                       商譽減值評估涉及重大判斷和估計，其中包
      assessment involves significant judgments and estimates which include the                      括採用適當的估值方法和使用關鍵假設（主要

(b)   Goodwill                                                                                 (b)   商譽

      Goodwill on acquisitions of subsidiaries is included in intangible assets. Goodwill is         收購子公司的商譽包含在無形資產中。商譽
      not amortised but it is tested for impairment annually, or more frequently if events           不 攤 銷，但 每 年 進 行 減 值 測 試，如 果 事 件 或
      or changes in circumstances indicate that it might be impaired, and is carried at              情況變化表明可能會減值，則會更頻繁地進
      cost less accumulated impairment losses. Gains and losses on the disposal of an                行 測 試，並 以 成 本 減 去 累 計 減 值 損 失 列 賬。
      entity include the carrying amount of goodwill relating to the entity sold.                    出售實體的損益包括與出售實體有關的商譽
                                                                                                     的賬面價值。

      Goodwill is allocated to cash-generating unit for the purpose of impairment                    商譽分配給現金產生單位以進行減值測試。
      testing. The allocation is made to those cash-generating unit (“CGU”) or                       分配給預期會從產生商譽的企業合併中受益
      groups of CGUs that are expected to benefit from the business combination in                   的那些現金產生單位或現金產生單位組。單
      which the goodwill arose. The units or groups of units are identified at the lowest            位或單位組在用於內部管理目的而監控商譽
      level at which goodwill is monitored for internal management purposes, being the               的最低級別進行識別，即經營分部（附註5）。
      operating segments (Note 5).

      The carrying amount of the CGU or group of CGUs including the allocated goodwill               包括已分配商譽在內的CGU或CGU組的賬面金
      is compared to the recoverable amount, which is the higher of value in use and                 額與可收回金額進行比較，可收回金額是使
      the fair value less costs of disposal. An impairment loss, being the amount by                 用價值和公允價值中較高者減去處置成本。
      which the carrying amount of the CGU or group of CGUs exceeded its recoverable                 任何減值一項減值損失，即某項或某組固定
      amount, is recognised immediately under “Other income and gains — net”.                        資產的賬面金額超過其可收回金額，應立即
                                                                                                     在「其他收入及利得 — 淨額」項下確認。

8     Intangible assets (Continued)                                                             8     無形資產（續）

(c)   Development costs                                                                         (c)   開發成本

      Research expenditure is recognised as an expense as incurred. Costs incurred                    研究開支在發生時確認為費用。當且僅當主
      on development projects (relating to the design and testing of new and improved                 體能符合以下所有條件時，項目開發成本（與
      software) are recognised as intangible assets when the following criteria are met:              設計及測試新開發軟件相關）相關支出應予確
                                                                                                      認為無形資產：

      –      it is technically feasible to complete the software so that it will be available         –   完成該軟件以致其可供使用在技術上是
             for use;                                                                                     可行的﹔

      –      management intends to complete the software and use or sell it;                          –   管理層有意完成該軟件並使用或出售﹔

      –      there is an ability to use or sell the software;                                         –   有能力使用或出售該軟件﹔

      –      it can be demonstrated how the software will generate probable future                    –   可證實該軟件如何產生很可能出現的未
             economic benefits;                                                                           來經濟利益﹔

      –      adequate technical, financial and other resources to complete the                        –   有足夠的技術、財務和其他資源完成開
             development and to use or sell the software are available; and                               發並使用或出售該軟件﹔及

      –      the expenditure attributable to the software during its development can be               –   該軟件在開發期內應佔的支出能可靠地
             reliably measured.                                                                           計量。

      Directly attributable costs that are capitalised include the software development               可直接資本化的成本包括軟件開發員工成本
      employee costs and an appropriate portion of relevant overheads.                                和相關管理費的適當部分。

      Other development expenditures that do not meet these criteria are recognised as                不符合以上條件的其他開發支出在產生時確
      an expense as incurred. Development costs previously recognised as an expense is                認為費用。以往確認為費用的開發成本不會
      not recognised as an asset in a subsequent period.                                              在往後期間確認為資產。

      Capitalised development costs are recorded as intangible assets and amortised                   資本化開發成本記錄為無形資產，並自該資
      from when the asset is ready for use on a straight-line basis over their estimated              產在其估計可使用年限（9個月至43個月）內可
      useful lives of 9 months to 43 months.                                                          以直線使用時開始攤銷。

      The loans to third parties represented loans made under the micro-credit business,            給予第三方的貸款為小額信貸業務產生的貸
      which bear interest at rates ranging from 8.00% to 24.00% (2024: 4.28% to                     款，貸款年利率在8.00%到24.00%（二零二四年：
      24.00%) per annum and are repayable with fixed terms agreed with the customers,               4.28%到24.00%）之間，並在與客戶商定的固定

      The fair values of the loans to third parties approximated their carrying amounts.            該給予第三方貸款的公允價值與其賬面價值
      The interest accrued was due within 12 months and presented in interest                       接近。本年計提的應收利息在12個月內到期，
      receivables.                                                                                  並列賬於應收利息中。

      The Group considers the probability of default upon initial recognition of asset and          本集團考慮在資產最初確認時發生違約的可
      whether there has been a significant increase in credit risk on an ongoing basis              能性，以及在整個報告期間信貸風險是否持
      throughout each reporting period. To assess whether there is a significant increase           續顯著增加。為了評估信用風險是否顯著增
      in credit risk, the Group compares the risk of a default occurring on the asset as at         加，本集團將資產在報告日期發生違約的風
      the reporting date with the risk of default as at the date of initial recognition.            險與初始確認日期發生違約的風險進行比較。

      The Group performed expected credit loss assessment of loans to third parties                 本集團通過對具有類似信用風險特徵的貸
      collectively by grouping loans with similar credit risk characteristics. During the           款進行分組，共同對第三方貸款進行了預期
      years ended 31 December 2025 and 2024, the majority of the loans were in stage 1              的信用損失評估。在截至二零二五年十二月
      and there was no significant change in credit quality for loans for both years.               三十一日和二零二四年十二月三十一日的年
                                                                                                    度 中，大 多 數 貸 款 處 於 第 一 階 段，並 且 這 兩
                                                                                                    年的貸款信用質量均未發生重大變化。

NOTES TO THE CONSOLIDATED FINANCIAL STATEMENTS
綜合財務報表附註



22    Deferred income tax(Continued)                                                              22   遞延所得稅（續）

      Deferred income tax is recognised, using the liability method, on temporary                      遞延所得稅採用負債法，對資產和負債的計
      differences arising between the tax bases of assets and liabilities and their carrying           稅基礎與其在綜合財務報表中的賬面價值之
      amounts in the consolidated financial statements. However, deferred tax liabilities              間 產 生 的 暫 時 性 差 異 予 以 確 認。但 是，如 果
      are not recognised if they arise from the initial recognition of goodwill, the deferred          遞延稅項負債是由於初始確認商譽而產生的，
      income tax is not accounted for if it arises from initial recognition of an asset or             則不予確認；如果遞延所得稅是由於在企業
      liability in a transaction other than a business combination that at the time of the             合併以外的交易中對資產或負債的初始確認
      transaction affects neither accounting nor taxable profit or loss and does not give              而 產 生 的，則 不 計 入 遞 延 所 得 稅。該 交 易 既
      rise to equal taxable and deductible temporary differences. Deferred income tax                  不影響會計也不影響應稅損益，且不會產生
      is determined using tax rates (and laws) that have been enacted or substantially                 相等的應納稅和可抵扣的暫時性差異。遞延
      enacted by the statement of financial position date and are expected to apply when               所得稅是使用在財務狀況表日期已經制定或
      the related deferred income tax asset is realised or the deferred income tax liability is        大致制定的稅率（和法律）確定的，預計在實
      settled.                                                                                         現相關遞延所得稅資產或清算遞延所得稅負
                                                                                                       債時適用。

      The deferred tax liability in relation to investment property that is measured at fair           與投資性物業有關的遞延所得稅負債，以公
      value is determined assuming the property will be recovered entirely through sale.               允價值計量，其確定前提是該物業將全部通
                                                                                                       過出售收回。

      Deferred tax assets are recognised only if it is probable that future taxable amounts            遞延稅項資產僅在很可能有未來應納稅額來
      will be available to utilise those temporary differences and losses.                             利用這些暫時性差異和損失時才予以確認。

      Deferred tax liabilities and assets are not recognised for temporary differences                 遞延所得稅負債和資產不能確認為國外業務
      between the carrying amount and tax bases of investments in foreign operations                   投資的賬面價值和稅基之間的暫時性差異，
      where the company is able to control the timing of the reversal of the temporary                 因為該公司能夠控制暫時性差異的轉回時間，
      differences and it is probable that the differences will not reverse in the foreseeable          並且該差異在可預見的未來很可能不會在轉
      future.                                                                                          回中反映。

      Deferred tax assets and liabilities are offset where there is a legally enforceable              遞延稅項資產和負債在具有法律上可強制執
      right to offset current tax assets and liabilities and where the deferred tax balances           行的權利以抵銷當期稅項資產和負債且遞延
      relate to the same taxation authority. Current tax assets and tax liabilities are offset         稅項餘額與同一稅務部門有關的情況下予以
      where the entity has a legally enforceable right to offset and intends either to settle          抵銷。當主體具有法律上可強制執行的抵銷
      on a net basis, or to realise the asset and settle the liability simultaneously.                 權並打算以淨額結算，或變現資產並同時清
                                                                                                       償負債時，則沖銷當期所得稅資產和稅項負債。

      Current and deferred tax is recognised in profit or loss, except to the extent that it           當期和遞延稅項在損益中確認，除非與在其
      relates to items recognised in other comprehensive income or directly in equity. In              他全面收益或直接在權益中確認的項目有關。
      this case, the tax is also recognised in other comprehensive income or directly in               在這種情況下，該稅項也分別在其他綜合收
      equity, respectively.                                                                            益中或直接在權益中確認。

(a)   Basic                                                                                   (a)   基本

      Basic earnings/(losses) per share is calculated by dividing the profit/(loss)                 基本每股盈利╱     （虧損）根據歸屬於本公司權
      attributable to owners of the Company by the weighted average number of ordinary              利 持 有 人 的 利 潤╱（虧 損），除 以 年 內 已 發 行
      shares in issue during the year, excluding ordinary shares purchased and held for             普通股的加權平均數目計算，但不包括為股
      share award plan.                                                                             份獎勵計劃而購買及持有的普通股。

36    Business combination                                                                  36    企業合併

      On 15 September 2025, the Group entered into an equity interest acquisition                 於二零二五年九月十五日，本集團訂立股權
      agreement to acquire an aggregate of 62.764% equity interest in CloudHub                    收 購 協 議，以 總 對 價 約 人 民 幣68,250,000元 收
      Network (Chongqing) Co., Ltd. (“CloudHub”) at a total consideration of                      購雲之家網絡（重慶）有限公司（「雲之家」             ）合
      approximately RMB68,250,000. CloudHub, headquartered in Chongqing, was                      計62.764%的 股 權。雲 之 家 總 部 位 於 重 慶，成
      established in 2014 and is a provider of enterprise collaboration and digital               立於2014年，是中國企業協作及數字化工作場
      workplace solutions in China. Details of the acquisition were disclosed in the              所解決方案提供商。本次收購詳情已載於本
      Company’s announcement dated 15 September 2025.                                             公司二零二五年九月十五日發佈的公告中。

      The acquisition was completed on 27 October 2025. Following the transaction,                該收購已於二零二五年十月二十七日完成。
      CloudHub became an indirect non-wholly-owned subsidiary of the Company, and                 交易完成後，雲之家成為本公司間接非全資
      its financial results have been consolidated into the Group’s financial statements          附屬公司，其財務業績自收購完成之日起已

(c)   Accounting policy                                                                       (c)   會計政策

      The acquisition method of accounting is used to account for all business                      不論權益工具或其他資產是否被收購，會計
      combinations, regardless of whether equity instruments or other assets are                    的購買法都被用於所有的企業合併。為收購
      acquired.                                                                                     附屬公司而轉讓的代價包括。


      The consideration transferred for the acquisition of a subsidiary comprises the:              收購子公司所轉讓之對價包括：


      •      fair values of the assets transferred                                                  •   轉讓資產的公允價值


      •      liabilities incurred to the former owners of the acquired business                     •   對被收購企業的前所有者承擔的責任


      •      equity interests issued by the Group                                                   •   集團發行的權益


      •      fair value of any asset or liability resulting from a contingent consideration         •   或有對價安排產生的任何資產或負債的
             arrangement, and                                                                           公允價值，以及


      •      fair value of any pre-existing equity interest in the subsidiary.                      •   子公司現有權益的公允價值


      Identifiable assets acquired and liabilities and contingent liabilities assumed in a          企業合併中取得的可辨認資產、負債和或有
      business combination are, with limited exceptions, measured initially at their fair           負債除個別情況外，最初按取得日的公允價
      values at the acquisition date. The Group recognises any non-controlling interest             值計量。本集團按按公允價值或按非控股權
      in the acquired entity on an acquisition-by-acquisition basis either at fair value or         益在被收購實體的可辨認淨資產中所佔的比
      at the non-controlling interest’s proportionate share of the acquired entity’s net            例，按逐筆收購的方式確認其在被收購實體
      identifiable assets.                                                                          中的任何非控股權益。

      If those amounts are less than the fair value of the net identifiable assets of the          譽。如果該等金額低於被收購企業的可辨認
      business acquired, the difference is recognised directly in profit or loss as a              資產淨值的公允價值，則差額直接確認為廉
      bargain purchase.                                                                            價收購的損益。

      Where settlement of any part of cash consideration is deferred, the amounts payable          如果現金對價的任何部分的結算被推遲，那
      in the future are discounted to their present value as at the date of exchange. The          麼將來應付的金額將按其在交換日的現值貼
      discount rate used is the entity’s incremental borrowing rate, being the rate at             現。使 用 的 貼 現 率 是 實 體 的 增 量 借 款 率，是
      which a similar borrowing could be obtained from an independent financier under              在可比的條款和條件下從獨立的融資人那裡
      comparable terms and conditions. Contingent consideration is classified either               獲得類似借款的比率。或有對價可分為權益
      as equity or a financial liability. Amounts classified as a financial liability are          或 財 務 負 債。被 歸 類 為 金 融 負 債 的 金 額，隨
      subsequently remeasured to fair value with changes in fair value recognised in               後會根據確認為損益的公允價值變動，重新
      profit or loss.                                                                              計量為公允價值。

      of subsidiaries acquired are individually businesses. If the fair value of total             一組活動和資產是否屬於單獨的業務。如果

      acquisition basis. For the non-controlling interests in the Group, the group elected         按逐筆收購的方式作出。就本集團的非控股

41.1 Principles of consolidation and equity accounting                                           41.1 合併及權益法會計原則

41.1.1 Subsidiaries                                                                              41.1.1子公司

      Subsidiaries are all entities (including structured entities) over which the Group has          子公司為本集團所控制的實體（包括結構化實
      control. The Group controls an entity where the Group is exposed to, or has rights              體）。當本集團從或有權從與該實體的參與中
      to, variable returns from its involvement with the entity and has the ability to affect         獲得變動收益並有能力對這些收益產生影響
      those returns through its power to direct the activities of the entity. Subsidiaries are        時，則 其 控 制 該 子 公 司。自 控 制 權 轉 移 至 本
      fully consolidated from the date on which control is transferred to the Group. They             集 團 之 日 起，子 公 司 被 合 併。其 自 喪 失 實 際
      are deconsolidated from the date that control ceases.                                           控制權之日起停止合併。

      The acquisition method of accounting is used to account for business                            本集團的業務合併採用會計購買法核算（見附
      combinations by the Group (refer to Note 36).                                                   註36）。

      Inter-company transactions, balances and unrealised gains on transactions                       集團內公司之間的交易、結餘及交易的未變
      between group companies are eliminated. Unrealised losses are also eliminated                   現 利 得 予 以 對 銷。未 變 現 損 失 亦 予 以 對 銷，
      unless the transaction provides evidence of an impairment of the transferred asset.             除非交易提供所轉撥資產的減值證據。子公
      Accounting policies of subsidiaries have been changed where necessary to ensure                 司報告的數額已按需要作出改變，以確保與
      consistency with the policies adopted by the Group.                                             本集團採用的政策符合一致。

      Non-controlling interests in the results and equity of subsidiaries are shown                   子公司業績和股權中的非控股權益分別列示
      separately in the consolidated income statement, consolidated statement of                      於 綜 合 利 潤 表、綜 合 全 面 收 益 表、綜 合 權 益
      comprehensive income, statement of changes in equity and consolidated statement                 變動表和綜合財務狀況表。
      of financial position respectively.

      control or joint control. This is generally the case where the Group holds between              聯 合 控 制 的 所 有 實 體。通 常 情 況 下，該 集 團
      20% and 50% of the voting rights. Investments in associates are accounted for                   擁 有20%至50%的 投 票 權。聯 營 公 司 的 投 資 在
      using the equity method of accounting (see Note 41.1.3 below), after initially being            最初按成本確認後，以權益會計方法核算（見
      recognised at cost.                                                                             附註41.1.3）。

41.1.3Equity method                                                                           41.1.3權益法

      Under the equity method of accounting, the investments are initially recognised at           權 益 法 下，投 資 賬 面 價 值 最 初 以 成 本 確 認，
      cost and adjusted thereafter to recognise the Group’s share of the post-acquisition          之後卻按本集團持有投資對象得份額確認投
      profits or losses of the investee in profit or loss, and the Group’s share of                資利潤或虧損以及其他綜合收益。從聯營公
      movements in other comprehensive income of the investee in other comprehensive               司收到或應收的股息確認為投資賬面價值的
      income. Dividends received or receivable from associates are recognised as a                 減少。
      reduction in the carrying amount of the investment.

      Where the Group’s share of losses in an equity-accounted investment equals                   若本集團在股本投資中所承擔的損失與本集
      or exceeds its interest in the entity, including any other unsecured long-term               團在該實體（包括任何其他無擔保的長期應收
      receivables, the Group does not recognise further losses, unless it has incurred             賬款）的權益相等或超過其權益，則本集團不
      obligations or made payments on behalf of the other entity.                                  承認進一步的損失，除非本集團已為該實體
                                                                                                   承擔了義務或代表該實體支付了款項。

      Unrealised gains on transactions between the Group and its associates are                    本集團與其聯營企業和合資企業之間的交易
      eliminated to the extent of the Group’s interest in these entities. Unrealised losses        的未變現收益，按本集團對這些實體的份額
      are also eliminated unless the transaction provides evidence of an impairment of             予以剔除。除非交易提供了轉讓資產減值的
      the asset transferred. Accounting policies of equity-accounted investees have been           證 據，否 則 未 變 現 虧 損 也 將 被 消 除。股 權 投
      changed where necessary to ensure consistency with the policies adopted by the               資公司的會計政策已根據需要進行了變更，
      Group.                                                                                       以確保與本集團所採取的會計政策保持一致。

      interests and any consideration paid or received is recognised in a separate reserve         已付或已收的任何代價之間的任何差額，將

                                                                                                   確認。

      or loss. This fair value becomes the initial carrying amount for the purposes of             為其公允價值，計入損益的賬面金額將發生
      subsequently accounting for the retained interest as an associate or financial asset.        變 化。該 公 允 價 值 成 為 作 為 聯 營 企 業、合 資
      In addition, any amounts previously recognised in other comprehensive income                 企業或金融資產的保留權益的後續會計目的
      in respect of that entity are accounted for as if the Group had directly disposed of         的 初 始 賬 面 價 值。此 外，任 何 先 前 在 有 關 該
      the related assets or liabilities. This may mean that amounts previously recognised          實體的其他綜合收入中確認的金額，在會計

      currency.

      exchange rates are generally recognised in profit or loss.                                   和損失在綜合利潤表確認。

      losses are presented in the income statement on a net basis within “Other gains/             兌利得和損失以淨額在綜合利潤表內的「其他
      losses – net”.                                                                               利得╱虧損 – 淨額」中列報。

      Non-monetary items that are measured at fair value in a foreign currency are                 非貨幣性金融資產及負債（例如以公允價值計
      translated using the exchange rates at the date when the fair value was determined.          量且其變動計入損益的權益）的折算差額列報

     Subsequent costs are included in the asset’s carrying amount or recognised as                   後續成本只有在很可能為本集團帶來與該項

     be measured reliably. The carrying amount of the replaced part is derecognised. All             為一項單獨資產。已更換零件的賬面值已被
     other repairs and maintenance are charged in the consolidated income statement                  終止確認。所有其他維修費用在產生的財政
     during the financial period in which they are incurred.                                         期間內於綜合利潤表支銷。

     Depreciation is calculated using the straight-line method to allocate their costs to            折舊採用以下的估計可使用年期將其成本按
     their residual values over their estimated useful lives, as follows:                            直線法分攤至其剩餘價值計算：

                                                                                                                            的較短者

     The assets’ residual values and useful lives are reviewed, and adjusted if                      資產的剩餘價值及可使用年期在每個財務狀
     appropriate, at each reporting period.                                                          況表日進行檢討，及在適當時調整。

     Construction in progress represents buildings under construction, which is stated               在建工程為在建工程，按實際建造成本減減
     at actual construction cost less any impairment loss. Construction in progress is               值損失列示。在建工程完成並準備投入使用
     transferred to property, plant and equipment when completed and ready for use.                  後，將轉入不動產、工廠及設備。

     (Note 41.21).

41.11 Borrowings                                                                                  41.11 借款

      Borrowings are recognised initially at fair value, net of transaction costs incurred.            一般及特定借款按公允價值並扣除產生的交
      Borrowings are subsequently stated at amortised cost; any differences between                    易費用為初始確認金額。借款其後按攤銷成
      proceeds (net of transaction cost) and the redemption value is recognised in the                 本列賬﹔所得款（扣除交易成本）與贖回價值
      income statement over the period of the borrowings using the effective interest                  的任何差額利用實際利率法於借款期間內在
      method.                                                                                          損益表確認。

      Fees paid on the establishment of loan facilities are recognised as transaction                  設立貸款融資時支付的費用倘部分或全部融
      costs of the loan to the extent that it is probable that some or all of the facility will        資將會很可能提取，該費用確認為貸款的交
      be drawn down. In this case, the fee is deferred until the draw-down occurs. To                  易 費 用。在 此 情 況 下，費 用 遞 延 至 貸 款 提 取
      the extent there is no evidence that it is probable that some or all of the facility will        為止。如沒有證據證明部分或全部融資將會
      be drawn down, the fee is capitalised as a pre-payment for liquidity services and                很可能被提取，則該費用資本化作為流動資
      amortised over the period of the facility to which it relates.                                   金服務的預付款，並按有關的融資期間攤銷。

      Borrowings are removed from the consolidated statement of financial position                     當 合 同 列 明 的 債 務 被 解 除、取 消 或 到 期 時，
      when the obligation specified in the contract is discharged, cancelled or expired.               借款自財務狀況表中刪除。已消除或轉撥至
      The difference between the carrying amount of a financial liability that has been                另一方的金融負債的賬面值與已付對價的差
      extinguished or transferred to another party and the consideration paid, including               額，包括任何已轉撥的非現金資產或承擔的
      any non-cash assets transferred or liabilities assumed, is recognised in profit or               負債，在損益中確認為其他收入或融資成本。
      loss as other income or finance costs.

      Borrowings are classified as current liabilities unless, at the end of the reporting             除非本集團於報告期末有權將負債的結算遞
      period, the Group has a right to defer settlement of the liability for at least 12               延 至 結 算 日 後 最 少12個 月，否 則 借 款 分 類 為
      months after the end of the reporting period.                                                    流動負債。

      Covenants that the Group is required to comply with, on or before the end of the                 在將附帶條款的貸款安排分類為流動或非流
      reporting period, are considered in classifying loan arrangements with covenants as              動時，會考慮本集團須於報告期末或之前遵
      current or non-current. Covenants that the Group is required to comply with after                守的條款。本集團須於報告期後遵守的條款
      the reporting period do not affect the classification at the reporting date.                     不會影響報告日期的分類。

41.12 Borrowing costs                                                                             41.12 借款成本

      General and specific borrowing costs directly attributable to the acquisition,                   直接歸屬於收購、興建或生產合資格資產（指
      construction or production of qualifying assets, which are assets that necessarily               必須經一段長時間處理以作其預定用途或銷
      take a substantial period of time to get ready for their intended use or sale, are               售的資產）的借款成本，加入該等資產的成本
      added to the cost of those assets, until such time as the assets are substantially               內，直至資產大致上備妥供其預定用途或銷
      ready for their intended use or sale.                                                            售為止。

      Investment income earned on the temporary investment of specific borrowings                      就特定借款，因有待合資格資產的支出而臨
      pending their expenditure on qualifying assets is deducted from the borrowing                    時投資賺取的投資收入，應自合資格資本化
      costs eligible for capitalisation.                                                               的借款成本中扣除。

      All other borrowing costs are recognised in profit or loss in the period in which                所有其他借款成本在產生期內的損益中確認。
      they are incurred.

      is recognised over the vesting period as an increase to investment in subsidiary              確 認，作 為 對 子 公 司 投 資 的 增 加，並 相 應 母
      undertakings, with a corresponding credit to equity in the parent entity accounts.            公司賬目的對權益貸記。

41.15 Provisions                                                                                41.15 準備

      Provisions are recognised when the Group has a present legal or constructive                    當本集團因已發生的事件而產生現有的法律
      obligation as a result of past events; it is probable that an outflow of resources will         或推定義務﹔很可能需要有資源的流出以結
      be required to settle the obligation; and the amount has been reliably estimated.               算 義 務﹔及 金 額 已 被 可 靠 估 計 時，當 作 出 準
      Provisions are not recognised for future operating losses.                                      備。準備不會就未來經營虧損確認準備。

      Where there are a number of similar obligations, the likelihood that an outflow will            如有多項類似義務，其需要在結算中有資源
      be required in settlement is determined by considering the class of obligations as a            流出的可能性，則可根據義務的類別整體考
      whole. A provision is recognised even if the likelihood of an outflow with respect to           慮。即使在同一義務類別所包含的任何一個
      any one item included in the same class of obligations may be small.                            項目相關的資源流出的可能性極低，仍須確
                                                                                                      認準備。

      Provisions are measured at the present value of management’s best estimate of                   撥備按管理層於報告期末償還現時義務所需
      the expenditure required to settle the present obligation at the end of the reporting           開支的最佳估計的現值計量。用於確定現值
      period. The discount rate used to determine the present value is a pre-tax rate                 的折現率是稅前利率，其反映了貨幣時間價
      that reflects current market assessments of the time value of money and the risks               值和負債特定風險的當前市場評估。隨著時
      specific to the liability. The increase in the provision due to the passage of time is          間的流逝，準備金的增加被確認為利息支出。
      recognised as interest expense.

41.16 Earnings per share                                                                        41.16 每股收益

(a)   Basic earnings per share                                                                  (a)   基本每股收益

      of the Company, excluding any costs of servicing equity other than ordinary                     佔利潤，不包括除普通股以外的任何股本服
      shares by the weighted average number of ordinary shares outstanding during the                 務成本，按本財政年度已發行普通股的加權
      financial year, adjusted for bonus elements in ordinary shares issued during the                平均數量計算，並根據當年發行的普通股中
      year and excluding treasury shares.                                                             的獎金因素進行調整，不包括庫存股。

(b)   Diluted earnings per share                                                                (b)   稀釋每股收益

      Diluted earnings per share adjusts the figures used in the determination of basic               稀釋每股收益調整用於確定每股基本收益的
      earnings per share to take into account:                                                        數位，以顧及：

      •       the after income tax effect of interest and other financing costs associated            •    利息及其他融資成本的稅後效應與稀釋
              with dilutive potential ordinary shares, and                                                 潛在普通股相關；

      •       the weighted average number of additional ordinary shares that would have               •    加權平均額外發行普通股的數量，假設
              been outstanding assuming the conversion of all dilutive potential ordinary                  所有稀釋性潛在普通股的轉換。
              shares.

41.18 Leases                                                                                  41.18 租賃

     Leases are recognised as a right-of-use asset and a corresponding liability at the            租賃在本集團可供租賃的資產使用當日確認
     date at which the leased asset is available for use by the Group.                             為使用權資產和相應的負債。

     Contracts may contain both lease and non-lease components. The Group allocates                合同可能同時包含租賃和非租賃組成部分。
     the consideration in the contract to the lease and non-lease components based on              本集團根據相對獨立價格將合同中的對價分
     their relative stand-alone prices. However, for leases of real estate for which the           配 給 租 賃 和 非 租 賃 部 分。然 而，對 於 本 集 團
     Group is a lessee, it has elected not to separate lease and non-lease components              為承租人的房地產租賃，它選擇不將租賃和
     and instead accounts for these as a single lease component.                                   非租賃組成部分分開，而是將其作為一個單
                                                                                                   獨的租賃組成部分進行會計處理。

     Lease terms are negotiated on an individual basis and contain a wide range of                 租賃條款是根據個人情況協商確定的，其中
     different terms and conditions. The lease agreements do not impose any covenants              包含各種不同的條款和條件。除了出租人持
     other than the security interests in the leased assets that are held by the lessor.           有的租賃資產中的擔保權益外，租賃協議不
     Leased assets may not be used as security for borrowing purposes.                             施加任何其他約定。租賃資產不得用作借貸
                                                                                                   擔保。

     Assets and liabilities arising from a lease are initially measured on a present               租賃產生的資產和負債按現值進行初始計量。
     value basis. Lease liabilities include the net present value of the following lease           租賃負債包括以下租賃付款額的淨現值：
     payments:

     •      fixed payments (including in-substance fixed payments), less any lease                 •     固定付款額（包括實質固定付款額），扣
            incentives receivable;                                                                       除任何應收的租賃激勵；

     •      variable lease payment that are based on an index or a rate, initially                 •     基於指數或比率確定的可變租賃付款
            measured using the index or rate as at the commencement date;                                額，採用租賃期開始日的指數或比率進
                                                                                                         行初始計量；

     •      amounts expected to be payable by the Group under residual value                       •     本集團根據餘值擔保預計應付的金額；
            guarantees;

     •      the exercise price of a purchase option if the Group is reasonably certain to          •     本集團合理確定將行使的購買選擇權的
            exercise that option, and                                                                    行權價格

     •      payments of penalties for terminating the lease, if the lease term reflects the        •     以及在租賃期反映出本集團將行使選擇
            Group exercising that option.                                                                權的情況下終止租賃的罰款金額。

     Lease payments to be made under reasonably certain extension options are also                 在合理的某些延期選擇權下支付的租賃付款
     included in the measurement of the liability.                                                 也包括在負債的計量中。

      The lease payments are discounted using the interest rate implicit in the lease. If           租賃付款使用租賃中隱含的利率折現。如果

      the lease, then the group entities use that rate as a starting point to determine the         賃情況與租賃的付款情況相似，則集團實體

      based on an index or rate, which are not included in the lease liability until they           租賃付款的潛在增加，直到生效後才包括在
      take effect. When adjustments to lease payments based on an index or rate take                租賃負債中。基於索引或費率的租賃付款調

      charged to profit or loss over the lease period so as to produce a constant periodic          成本在租賃期內從損益中扣除，以使每個期
      rate of interest on the remaining balance of the liability for each period.                   間的負債餘額產生固定的定期利率。

      The recoverable amount is the higher of an asset’s fair value less costs of disposal         確認減值損失。可收回金額是資產的公允價
      and value in use. For the purposes of assessing impairment, assets are grouped               值減去處置費用和使用價值中的較高者。為

      are largely independent of the cash inflows from other assets or groups of assets            的現金流入的最低級別，這些現金流入在很
      (cash-generating unit). Non-financial assets other than goodwill that suffered an            大程度上獨立於來自其他資產或資產組（現金
